Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on hyperbolic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 162. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 386 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by hyperbolic generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 386. QED.
